CoMLP models cross-modal dependencies through cooperative cross-gating, built upon complementary regional and dilated MLP interactions, to capture local and global cross-modal dependencies. We further develop a multi-source fusion architecture in which CoMLP performs both inter-image fusion across imaging modalities and vision-language fusion between visual features and textual reports, enabling heterogeneous information to be integrated without relying on dense cross-attention. Extensive experiments on five medical segmentation benchmarks, covering 2D/3D images, clinical reports, multiple imaging modalities, and diverse anatomical regions, demonstrate consistent improvements over state-of-the-art multi-modal and language-guided segmentation methods. Ablation studies further show that fine-grained interaction at high spatial resolutions and complementary local-global fusion are critical to the performance gains. These results demonstrate the potential of MLP-based interaction as an effective alternative for fine-grained cross-modal information fusion in medical image segmentation.

</details>


### [34] [CLON: Cue-Calibrated Linguistic Object Onboarding for Zero-Shot 6D Pose Front-Ends](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04784)
*Seojin Ji,Yoojin Kwon,Hyung-Sin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CLON是一个零样本6D姿态估计的检测前端方法，通过构建语言语义记忆和对象集线索权重来提升物体提准质量，在七个BOP数据集上显著超越CNOS和SAM-6D基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本6D姿态估计pipeline通常依赖强大的下游姿态求解器，但性能受限于前端（object proposals）：前端必须在保留部分遮挡的真实正样本的同时，拒绝语义上看似合理的干扰物。作者希望设计一个无需针对新物体进行任务特定训练的前端，以解决这个瓶颈。

Method: CLON由两部分组成：(1) 给定已注册对象集的渲染模板，构建一个语言语义记忆（linguistic semantic memory），用于指导SAM 3生成高召回率的物体提议；(2) 从对象集中一次性计算对象集线索权重（cue weights），在场景推理前完成计算，在线打分时保持固定，用于校准提议评分。

Result: 在七个BOP-Classic-Core数据集上，CLON相比CNOS和SAM-6D前端，检测AP提升8.1个百分点，分割AP提升6.2个百分点，下游6D姿态AR最高提升4.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过语言语义与视觉线索的校准结合，CLON有效解决了零样本物体检测前端在召回正样本与抑制干扰之间的权衡，是一种无需任务特定训练即可提升整体6D姿态估计pipeline性能的有效方案。

Abstract: Zero-shot 6D pose estimation pipelines increasingly rely on strong downstream pose solvers, but their performance is often limited by the front-end: object proposals must preserve partially visible true positives while rejecting semantically plausible distractors. We introduce Cue-Calibrated Linguistic Object Onboarding (CLON), a front-end requiring no task-specific training for new objects. Given rendered templates of the onboarded object set, CLON constructs a linguistic semantic memory for top-down proposal generation and object-set cue weights for calibrated proposal scoring. The linguistic memory guides SAM 3 toward high-recall proposals for onboarded objects, while cue weights are computed once from the onboarded object set before scene inference and kept fixed during online scoring. On seven BOP-Classic-Core datasets, CLON improves detection AP by 8.1 percentage points (pp), segmentation AP by 6.2 pp, and downstream 6D pose AR by up to 4.1 pp over CNOS and SAM-6D front-ends.

</details>


### [35] [An Attention-Guided Global and Local Fusion Framework for Lesion-Focused Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04791)
*Mst Shafia Tasnima,Md Samaun Elaheea,Tanjim Taharat Aurpab,Md Musfique Anwar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种三分支注意力引导的深度学习框架（基于DenseNet-121），通过自适应融合全局上下文信息与病灶局部信息，提升病灶聚焦图像分类的性能与可解释性。在合成斑点数据集及皮肤病变、番石榴叶、葡萄叶三个基准数据集上验证，融合分支优于单独的全局或局部分支，皮肤病变数据集准确率达97.75%，番石榴叶数据集达99.64%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病灶聚焦图像分类中判别信号稀疏、空间分散且易被背景噪声掩盖，传统CNN均匀处理整幅图像会稀释信号相关性。作者假设自适应融合全局上下文信息与病灶聚焦的局部信息能比单独使用任一表示获得更好的分类性能。

Method: 基于DenseNet-121构建三分支注意力引导框架：(1) 全局分支从完整图像学习表示；(2) 使用Grad-CAM生成注意力图，高亮预测相关区域并生成掩码输入；(3) 局部分支集成CBAM模块，从聚焦区域提取细化的空间和通道特征；(4) 自适应融合分支通过学习实例级权重，动态整合全局与局部表征。

Result: 在SSPD合成数据集及皮肤病变、番石榴叶、葡萄叶三个基准数据集上评估，融合分支均优于单独使用全局或局部分支。皮肤病变数据集准确率达97.75%，番石榴叶数据集达99.64%。

Conclusion: 注意力引导架构价值显著：能提升模型透明度、增强特征相关性、支持更可靠的数据驱动决策，在医疗图像分析领域有重要应用价值。

Abstract: Lesion-focused image classification presents a core analytical challenge, as discriminative signals are often sparse, spatially dispersed, and easily obscured by background noise, while conventional convolutional neural networks (CNNs) process entire images uniformly and may dilute signal relevance. This study hypothesizes that adaptive fusion of global contextual information and lesion-focused local information can improve classification performance compared with using either representation independently. We propose a three-branch, attention-guided deep learning framework built on Densely Connected Convolutional Network-121 (DenseNet-121) to improve feature attribution, interpretability, and classification reliability. The architecture consists of a global branch that learns representations from full images, followed by Gradient-weighted Class Activation Mapping (Grad-CAM) to generate attention maps that highlight prediction-relevant regions and produce masked inputs, and a local branch enhanced with a Convolutional Block Attention Module (CBAM) to extract refined spatial and channel-wise features from these focused regions. An adaptive fusion branch integrates global and local representations by learning instance-specific weights, allowing dynamic prioritization between contextual and localized information. The framework is evaluated on a synthetic Spot Pattern Dataset (SSPD) and three benchmark datasets, including skin lesion, guava leaf, and grape leaf image datasets, where the fusion branch outperformed the individual global and local branches, reaching 97.75% accuracy on the skin lesion dataset and 99.64% on the guava leaf dataset. The results highlight the value of attention-guided architectures in healthcare analytics by improving model transparency, strengthening feature relevance, and supporting more reliable data-driven decision-making in medical image analysis.

</details>


### [36] [Intrinsic Temporal Adaptation of CLIP for Partially Relevant Video Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04800)
*Hyun Seok Seong,Woojin Jun,SuBeen Lee,Jae-Pil Heo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ITA框架用于部分相关视频检索（PRVR），通过骨干网络内部时间适配（让视觉Transformer最后几层关注相邻帧组）和亲和力加权梯度传播（基于文本-帧亲和力软聚合top-k帧），在不微调CLIP的前提下获得具有时间感知的帧嵌入，实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PRVR需要基于细粒度帧级理解进行检索，但现有方法依赖冻结的CLIP帧特征，缺乏时间理解能力；即使参数高效CLIP适配方法取得进展，视频级别的预测仍可能由不精确的帧级证据支撑，无法准确定位查询相关的时刻。

Method: 提出ITA框架，包含两个核心组件：(1) 骨干网络内部时间适配（Backbone-Internal Temporal Adaptation），使视觉Transformer最后几层能跨相邻帧组进行注意力计算，在保持CLIP冻结的前提下仅训练适配参数，生成具有时间感知的帧嵌入；(2) 亲和力加权梯度传播（Affinity-Weighted Gradient Propagation），针对PRVR的弱监督特性，基于文本-帧亲和力对top-k帧进行软聚合，将学习信号传播到多个与查询相关的帧上。

Result: 在PRVR基准上取得SOTA性能，表现出鲁棒的跨数据集迁移能力，并在真实查询相关时刻内检索到明显更准确的帧级证据。

Conclusion: 通过时间感知的帧嵌入与弱监督下的梯度传播机制，ITA有效解决了PRVR中粗粒度匹配与精确证据定位之间的矛盾，证明了参数高效CLIP适配在视频时间理解上的可行性。

Abstract: Partially Relevant Video Retrieval (PRVR) aims to retrieve untrimmed videos that contain moments relevant to a text query. Since the target moment occupies only a portion of the video, PRVR requires retrieval based on fine-grained understanding beyond coarse video-level matching. However, existing methods often rely on frozen CLIP frame features, which lack temporal understanding. Even with recent progress in parameter-efficient CLIP adaptation, video-level predictions can still be supported by imprecise frame-level evidence. In this paper, we propose an Intrinsic Temporal Adaptation (ITA) framework for PRVR. First, our Backbone-Internal Temporal Adaptation allows the last few visual transformer layers to attend over groups of neighboring frames. This provides temporally aware frame embeddings while keeping CLIP frozen and training only adaptation parameters. Second, we introduce Affinity-Weighted Gradient Propagation to address the weakly supervised nature of PRVR, softly aggregating top-$k$ frames based on text-frame affinities and propagating learning signals to multiple query-relevant frames. Our method achieves state-of-the-art performance on PRVR benchmarks, demonstrates robust cross-dataset transfer, and retrieves substantially more accurate frame-level evidence within ground-truth query-relevant moments. Our code is available at github.com/hynnsk/ITA.

</details>


### [37] [Linguistic Trajectory Encoding for Efficient Long-Horizon Spatial Memory in Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04802)
*Tianyidan Xie,Shenyi Wang,Qiang Tang,Mingjie Wang,Zhicheng Qiu,Xuanfu Li,Zhan Xu,Jian Yang,Lanjun Wang,Zili Yi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出语言轨迹编码（LTE）方法，用于具身智能体在长时程任务中对动态对象的状态转换进行可语言查询的记忆表示。LTE通过自然语言描述、稀疏空间锚点和视觉锚点的混合表示压缩动态对象运动历史，并在EgoLife构建的空间记忆基准（SMB）上分别达到45.3%和48.7%的语义轨迹检索与长时程对象检索成功率，同时实现8.7倍至26.1倍的轨迹压缩与亚秒级查询延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身智能体执行长时程任务时，需要在小时到天级别的观察范围内，以自然语言形式查询动态对象的状态转换记忆。现有系统各有局限：基于剪辑级的视频-语言嵌入会丢失细粒度运动信息，几何SLAM仅保留原始坐标，智能体工作记忆围绕当前任务情境组织。三者均无法提供每个对象的、状态转换本身可语言查询的时间线，亟需新的记忆表示方案。

Method: LTE采用混合表示压缩动态对象运动历史：结合自然语言描述、稀疏空间锚点和视觉锚点。压缩策略自适应运动复杂度——在没有可靠观测的时段将对象锚定到最后出现的位置，而在运动过程中用几何路标和语言描述表示以保持精度。为评估跨长时间范围的能力，作者从EgoLife多日记录构建了空间记忆基准（SMB），覆盖语义轨迹检索和长时程对象检索两项现有基准缺失的能力。

Result: 在SMB上，LTE系统语义轨迹检索成功率45.3%，长时程对象检索48.7%，优于结构化记忆与VLM基线（此前最好成绩分别为31.9%和34.4%）。轨迹压缩因子为8.7倍至26.1倍，在24小时视频上查询延迟低于1秒。在Ego4D自然语言查询上达到28.75%/55.10%的R@1/R@5，较EgoVLPv2提升15.80/31.30个百分点。

Conclusion: LTE表明通过语言-空间-视觉混合锚定的轨迹编码，能够在大幅压缩存储在数据前提下保持对象运动历史的语言可查询性，显著优于现有结构化记忆与VLM基线，为长时程具身记忆提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Embodied agents performing long-horizon tasks require a memory representation in which the state transitions of dynamic objects remain queryable in natural language across hours-to-days observation horizons. Existing systems either drop fine-grained motion (clip-level video-language embeddings), keep it only as raw coordinates (geometric SLAM), or organise it around immediate task context (agent working memories). None of them gives the agent a per-object timeline whose state transitions are themselves queryable in language. Our key contribution is \textbf{Linguistic Trajectory Encoding} (LTE), which compresses dynamic object motion histories via a hybrid representation combining natural language descriptions, sparse spatial anchors, and visual anchors. LTE adapts compression to motion complexity by anchoring periods without reliable observations to the last seen location, while representing motion with geometric waypoints and linguistic descriptions to preserve accuracy. To evaluate these capabilities across extended time horizons, we construct the \textbf{Spatial Memory Benchmark} (SMB) from EgoLife multi-day recordings, targeting capabilities absent in existing benchmarks: semantic trajectory retrieval and long-horizon object retrieval. On SMB, the LTE-based system achieves $45.3\%$ success in semantic trajectory retrieval and $48.7\%$ in long-horizon object retrieval, outperforming structured-memory and VLM baselines (best prior: $31.9\%$ and $34.4\%$). LTE achieves trajectory compression by factors of $8.7\times$ to $26.1\times$ with sub-second query latency on $24$\,h video. On Ego4D natural-language queries, the system reaches $28.75\%$ / $55.10\%$ R@1/R@5, $+15.80$ / $+31.30$ pts over EgoVLPv2.

</details>


### [38] [Weather-Conditioned Depth Anything](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04827)
*Zhaoming Xu,Chan-Wei Hu,Kuan-Ru Huang,Zihao Zhu,Renjie Li,Yang Zhou,Zhengzhong Tu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DA-W是一个面向恶劣天气（雾、雨、雪、低光）的鲁棒单目深度估计框架，通过风格过滤器提取内容无关的天气嵌入，并以参数高效的零初始化适配器注入Depth Anything骨干网络，实现统一模型在多种天气条件下的鲁棒深度估计，同时不遗忘正常条件下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单目深度估计基础模型（如Depth Anything系列）在多种领域表现优异，但在恶劣天气条件下（雾、雨、雪、夜间）仍存在严重的性能退化。因此需要一种能够显式解耦风格与内容的方法，使模型对环境退化具有鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 训练一个风格过滤器（Style Filter）在真实与合成退化数据的混合集上提取内容无关、感知退化类型的天气嵌入（weather embeddings）；2) 通过参数高效、零初始化的适配器将该风格嵌入注入Depth Anything骨干网络；3) 使用伪标签蒸馏和对齐策略训练适配器；4) 单一模型即可同时适应雾、雨、雪和低光等多种环境条件。

Result: 在自建天气基准上AbsRel平均提升3.7%，达到最先进的鲁棒深度估计性能；在标准干净基准上性能与原有模型持平或略有提升，证明未发生灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: DA-W通过风格与内容的显式解耦和轻量级条件调制，实现了单一统一模型在正常与恶劣天气条件下的鲁棒深度估计，验证了天气风格条件化方法的有效性。

Abstract: Monocular depth estimation foundation models, such as the Depth Anything series, have achieved remarkable performance across diverse domains. However, they still suffer from critical failures under adverse weather conditions, such as fog, rain, snow, or at night. To address this, we present Weather-Conditioned Depth Anything (DA-W), a framework that explicitly disentangles style from content for weather-robust depth estimation. Specifically, we introduce a Style Filter trained on a curated mix of real and synthetic degradation datasets to extract content-independent, degradation-aware weather embeddings. This style embedding is then injected into the Depth Anything backbone using a parameter-efficient, zero-initialized adapter. Such a lightweight modulation allows a single unified model to robustly adapt to diverse conditions, including fog, rain, snow, and low-light, while avoiding catastrophic forgetting of its core generalization abilities in normal conditions. We train the adapter using a pseudo-label distillation and alignment strategy. Our comprehensive experiments demonstrate that our proposed DA-W achieves state-of-the-art robust depth estimation, improving AbsRel by an average of 3.7% on our curated weather benchmarks, while matching or slightly outperforming performance on standard clean benchmarks. Our project page is available at https://zhaoming-tamu.github.io/WCDA/.

</details>


### [39] [PAPT++: Risk-Aware Adversarial Tuning and Generation for Single Domain Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04837)
*Zhipeng Xu,De Cheng,Xinyang Jiang,Lingfeng He,Huaijie Wang,Dongsheng Li,Nannan Wang,Xinbo Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PAPT++是一个面向单域泛化(SDG)的任务感知对抗生成-训练框架，利用分布稳健优化(DRO)思想在预训练文本到图像(T2I)扩散模型的类条件生成空间中搜索高分类损失的困难样本，通过语义参考图像引导生成与分类器交替更新，渐进式地使分类器接触具有挑战性且语义一致的分布变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单域泛化旨在仅从单个标注源域学习模型并泛化到未见目标域。现有常见策略是利用数据增强或T2I扩散模型扩充源域分布，但仅靠多样性不足以保证稳健泛化——有用的生成样本还应捕捉当前分类器难以应对的变化，即需要风险感知而非单纯多样化的样本生成。

Method: PAPT++受DRO启发，在预训练T2I模型的类条件生成空间中定义语义模糊集，并搜索当前分类器下分类损失较高的样本。具体包括：(1)通过学习多样化的类语义参考图像（借助图像文本对齐和类内多样性正则化）作为去噪目标，减少语义漂移并引导生成困难变化；(2)将生成样本与源数据联合更新分类器，更新后的分类器再指导下一轮合成。二者交替进行，实现风险感知的对抗式生成训练框架。

Result: 在标准SDG基准上的大量实验表明，PAPT++方法优于现有方法，且各主要组件的有效性得到验证。

Conclusion: PAPT++通过将生成样本的筛选从"多样性"升级为"分类困难度感知"，将分类器与扩散生成器协同对抗训练，有效提升了单域泛化性能。

Abstract: Single domain generalization (SDG) aims to learn a model from one labeled source domain that generalizes to unseen target domains. A common strategy is to enrich the source distribution with augmented or generated samples, and recent text-to-image (T2I) diffusion models provide a strong generative prior for this purpose. However, diversity alone is insufficient for robust generalization, because useful generated samples should also capture variations that the current classifier finds difficult. Motivated by distributionally robust optimization (DRO), we define a semantic ambiguity set in the class-conditional generative space of a pretrained T2I model and search it for samples with high classification loss under the current classifier. To this end, we introduce PAPT++, a risk-aware adversarial generation-training framework for SDG. PAPT++ first learns diverse semantic reference images for each class through image-text alignment and intra-class diversity regularization. These references then serve as denoising targets during classifier-guided diffusion synthesis, reducing semantic drift while guiding generation toward challenging variations. The generated samples are combined with the source data to update the classifier, and the updated classifier guides the next synthesis round in return. In this way, PAPT++ progressively exposes the classifier to challenging yet semantically consistent variations. Extensive experiments on standard SDG benchmarks demonstrate the superiority of the proposed PAPT++ method and the effectiveness of its main components.

</details>


### [40] [LetOccVote: Learning Weakly Supervised 3D Occupancy through Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04846)
*Chi Zhang,Qi Song,Feifei Li,Jie Li,Rui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LetOccVote，一个基于高斯的弱监督3D占用预测框架，利用跨帧投票机制同时改进几何和语义伪标签的质量。通过Depth Vote利用跨帧几何一致性优化深度伪标签，通过Semantic Vote在共享3D空间中聚合伪语义观测以筛选可靠信息，仅用2D伪标签监督即可在Occ3D-nuScenes上达到SOTA性能（53.27 IoU，20.39 mIoU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弱监督3D占用预测通过2D伪标签避免昂贵的3D标注，但现有方法直接使用不完美的伪标签作为监督，导致占用学习易受错误的几何和语义目标影响。作者观察到跨帧观测的一致性可作为评估伪标签可靠性的廉价而有效的信号，因此希望利用这一线索改进监督质量。

Method: 提出LetOccVote框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) Depth Vote：利用跨帧几何一致性来精炼受支持的伪深度并剔除矛盾估计，在进行体积提升和深度监督之前完成；(2) Semantic Vote：在共享3D空间聚合伪语义观测，区分可靠与有争议的证据，增强可靠语义监督并过滤不可靠的伪标签片段。整体训练仅需2D伪标签监督，无需3D占用标注。

Result: 在Occ3D-nuScenes数据集上，LetOccVote达到53.27 IoU和20.39 mIoU，在2D伪标签监督方法中取得最先进性能。

Conclusion: 验证了跨帧一致性投票可以有效缓解弱监督3D占用预测中伪标签噪声问题，在无需3D标注的条件下显著提升几何和语义占用预测质量。

Abstract: Weakly supervised 3D occupancy prediction reduces the reliance on costly 3D annotations by learning from 2D pseudo-labels generated by vision foundation models. However, existing methods typically use these imperfect pseudo-labels directly as supervision, making occupancy learning vulnerable to erroneous geometric and semantic targets. We observe that agreement across repeated observations provides an inexpensive and reliable cue for assessing pseudo-label reliability. Based on this observation, we propose \textbf{LetOccVote}, a weakly supervised Gaussian-based occupancy framework that leverages cross-frame voting to improve both geometric and semantic supervision. For geometry, Depth Vote exploits cross-frame geometric agreement to refine supported pseudo depth and reject contradictory estimates before volumetric lifting and depth supervision. For semantics, Semantic Vote aggregates pseudo-semantic observations in a shared 3D space to identify reliable and contested evidence, strengthening reliable semantic supervision while filtering unreliable pseudo-label segments. The entire framework is trained solely with 2D pseudo-label supervision without requiring 3D occupancy annotations. On Occ3D-nuScenes, LetOccVote achieves 53.27 IoU and 20.39 mIoU, establishing state-of-the-art performance among methods with 2D pseudo-label supervision.

</details>


### [41] [Mitigating Performance Discrepancy in Cross-Domain 3D Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04860)
*Jinge Ma,Gautham Vinod,Bruce Coburn,Jui-Feng Chi,Siddeshwar Raghavan,Fengqing Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统地探讨了3D点云分类增量学习（CIL）中由数据域异质性（如干净CAD、RGB-D扫描、视频重建、损坏观测等）带来的"性能差异"（performance discrepancy）问题，提出了专门的Domain3D-CIL评估协议，并设计了无范例（exemplar-free）方法PolyMem，通过学习特征分布的高阶统计量来增强跨域鲁棒性并缓解性能差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D感知在真实应用中至关重要，但实际中模型需要持续适应新出现的3D对象类别。与2D图像不同，3D点云天然异质——同类对象可来自干净CAD域、不同质量的RGB-D相机扫描、视频重建甚至损坏观测。作者发现这种异质性除了引发传统的灾难性遗忘外，还引入了新挑战：跨域的性能退化程度差异显著，即"性能差异"（performance discrepancy）问题，这一问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 1) 建立Domain3D-CIL训练与评估协议，包含来自异构域的点云类别数据；2) 将多种主流CIL方法适配到3D模态进行基准测试；3) 提出PolyMem——一种无范例（exemplar-free）方法，通过隐式建模特征分布的高阶统计量来增强跨域鲁棒性。

Result: 实验表明性能差异现象在各类主流CIL基线方法中一致性地出现；PolyMem方法有效缓解了性能差异问题，同时提升了模型在各域上的整体性能。

Conclusion: 作者揭示了3D CIL中一个此前未被关注的"性能差异"新挑战，并证明了跨域异质性对增量学习的影响不容忽视。PolyMem作为无范例方法，通过高阶统计量建模有效缓解了该问题，为3D持续学习提供了新的研究方向和可行解决方案。

Abstract: 3D perception plays a crucial role in real-world applications such as autonomous driving, robotics, and AR/VR. In practical scenarios, 3D perception models need to continually adapt to newly emerging 3D object categories, making class-incremental learning (CIL) particularly important. However, unlike 2D images, 3D point clouds are inherently heterogeneous: objects from the same class may not only come from the clean CAD domain, but also from RGB-D camera scans of varying quality, video reconstructions, or even corrupted observations. We discover that such heterogeneity introduces a new challenge beyond catastrophic forgetting: the degree of performance degradation can vary substantially across domains, a phenomenon we term performance discrepancy. To investigate this problem, we establish the Domain3D-CIL training and evaluation protocol, which contains point cloud categories from heterogeneous domains. We further adapt a wide range of mainstream CIL methods to the 3D modality. The results demonstrate that this performance discrepancy consistently appears across these baselines. To mitigate this issue, we introduce PolyMem, an exemplar-free approach that implicitly models rich high-order statistics of the feature distribution to enhance cross-domain robustness. Experiments demonstrate that our method effectively alleviates the performance discrepancy while improving the model's performance across domains. Code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [42] [SimFuse3D: Source-Guided Target Simulation and Confidence-Guided Multi-Stage Localization Reweighting for Cross-Platform 3D Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04886)
*Yongchun Lin,Xinliang Zhang,Yun Zou,Zhixuan Xiao,Liang Lei,Jianya Guo,Yuqiang Zhai,Xiaofeng Wang,HaiKuo Xu,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SimFuse3D是一种跨平台LiDAR无监督域适应方法，通过保留目标位置并利用标注源扫描的实测几何修复伪目标，解决box-point不一致性问题，在六个跨平台迁移任务中全面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器高度和视角变化会导致跨平台LiDAR数据的点级分布不同，使无监督域适应变得困难。自训练中保留的预测虽能提供有效目标位置，但可能包裹稀疏前景、背景杂波或与预测框不一致的点，即box-point不一致性问题，影响伪标签质量与适应效果。

Method: 提出SimFuse3D框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) Object Memory检索兼容的标注源实例；(2) Target Simulation将源实例的真实框放置在目标位置，按目标观测几何对齐点云并过滤裁剪以近似目标观测；(3) Confidence-Guided Multi-Stage Localization Reweighting (CMLR)将伪目标置信度映射为有界权重，供RPN定位和R-CNN框回归共享。所有组件仅在适应阶段运行，不改变检测器架构与推理图。

Result: 在六个跨平台迁移任务中，SimFuse3D在所有报告的AP指标上均超越Pi3DET-Net，并在几乎所有指标上排名第一。在nuScenes-to-KITTI任务中，使用两种评估检测器均排名第一。

Conclusion: SimFuse3D通过几何实测修复解决box-point不一致问题，有效提升跨平台LiDAR UDA性能，且无需修改检测器架构，具有较好的通用性。

Abstract: Changes in sensor height and viewpoint alter object-level point distributions, making cross-platform LiDAR unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) difficult. Self-training uses labeled source scans and unlabeled target scans, yet a retained prediction may provide a useful target location while enclosing sparse foreground returns, background clutter, or points inconsistent with the predicted box. We refer to this mismatch as box-point inconsistency. We introduce SimFuse3D, which preserves the target placement and repairs the associated pseudo-object using measured geometry from labeled source scans. Object Memory retrieves a compatible labeled source instance. Target Simulation places its ground-truth box at the target location, aligns its points with the target viewing geometry, and filters the aligned crop to approximate the target observation. Confidence-Guided Multi-Stage Localization Reweighting (CMLR) maps each target pseudo-object confidence score to a bounded weight shared by RPN localization and R-CNN box regression. All components operate only during adaptation, leaving the detector architecture and inference graph unchanged. Across six cross-platform transfers, SimFuse3D exceeds Pi3DET-Net on every reported AP metric and ranks first among the compared adaptation methods on nearly all metrics. On nuScenes-to-KITTI, it ranks first among the compared adaptation methods with both evaluated detectors.

</details>


### [43] [Sound-based Multi-Person 3D Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04902)
*Yusuke Oumi,Yuto Shibata,Go Irie,Akisato Kimura,Yoshimitsu Aoki,Mariko Isogawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次尝试仅利用声学信号估计多人3D姿态，提出了SoundMHPE编码器-解码器框架，并构建了包含6小时、432K同步帧的声学多人姿态数据集AMP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人声学信号存在叠加的运动相关信号变化以及人体间反射引入的复杂传播延迟，导致难以将特定信号变化归因于某个个体的姿态，使得多人3D姿态估计极具挑战性。

Method: 提出SoundMHPE框架，包含两个核心组件：Acoustic Multi-scale Encoder捕获多样化的时间与细粒度频率特征以从复杂重叠信号中分离细微声学签名；Temporal Pose Decoder利用注意力机制在连续帧间解耦多人信息，联合建模时间动态与个体间依赖以精确重建逐帧个体姿态。

Result: 构建了6小时、包含432K同步帧多人姿态与声学数据的AMP数据集，实验表明SoundMHPE优于基线模型。

Conclusion: 通过多尺度编码与时间注意力解码相结合，SoundMHPE有效解决了多人声学信号重叠与反射干扰问题，证明仅依靠声音恢复多人3D姿态的可行性。

Abstract: Can we recover the 3D poses of multiple people using only sound? This paper presents the first attempt to estimate multi-person 3D poses solely from acoustic signals. Estimating the poses of multiple individuals using acoustic signals is inherently challenging due to the superposition of motion-dependent signal variations. Unlike single-person scenarios, the presence of multiple subjects leads to overlapping acoustic signatures, making it difficult to attribute specific signal changes to an individual's pose. Furthermore, the complexity is compounded by inter-person reflections, which introduce intricate propagation delays that obscure the temporal motion-acoustic relationship. To address these issues, we propose SoundMHPE (Sound-based Multi-person Human Pose Estimator), a novel encoder-decoder framework consisting of two key components. First, the Acoustic Multi-scale Encoder captures diverse temporal and fine-grained frequency features to isolate subtle acoustic signatures from complex, overlapping signals. Second, the Temporal Pose Decoder employs an attention mechanism to disentangle multi-person information across successive frames. By jointly accounting for temporal dynamics and inter-person dependencies, this component precisely reconstructs frame-wise individual poses. To validate our approach, we constructed the 6-hour Acoustic Multi-person Pose (AMP) dataset consisting of 432K synchronized frames of multi-person pose and acoustic data, and demonstrated that our SoundMHPE outperforms baseline models. Project page: https://oumi03.github.io/sound-mhpe/

</details>


### [44] [InterSing: Explicit Interaction Dynamics for 3D Duet Singing Animation and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04903)
*Yihan Zhou,Zikai Huang,Yuyang Yu,Xuemiao Xu,Cheng Xu,Shengfeng He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterSing是一个用于生成双人歌唱表演3D头部动画的框架，通过引入"交互逻辑量"(interaction logits)这一时间变化信号来建模表演者之间的互动强度，并用其条件化一个由音频特征和交互动力学共同驱动的扩散模型，实现从独立动作到协调行为的统一多模式生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双人歌唱表演中，每位歌手需要在保持个人表现力的同时，在音乐关键节点（如乐句边界、节奏同步、应答段落）进行间歇性互动。由于这些互动稀疏且依赖节奏，现有音频驱动的动画方法和对话交互模型都无法充分捕捉其结构。

Method: 核心洞见是将双人协调表示为随时间变化的信号，反映表演者在整首歌中的互动强度。基于此引入交互逻辑量(interaction logits)这一可解释的潜在表示，通过弱监督学习获得，并用于条件化一个交互感知的扩散模型，该模型由音频特征和交互动力学联合驱动。

Result: 实验证明InterSing生成的双人歌唱头部动画在协调性和音乐对齐方面优于现有方法，同时保留每位表演者的特征性动作风格。该框架还能泛化到多歌手表演场景，并提供对互动时机和方式的直观控制。

Conclusion: InterSing通过将交互建模为可学习的时间信号，成功实现了双人（甚至多人）歌唱中独立动作与协调行为的统一生成，既提升了协调性和音乐对齐度，又保持了个人动作风格的可控性与可解释性。

Abstract: We present InterSing, a framework for generating realistic 3D head animations for duet singing performances. Unlike solo singing, duet performance requires each singer to balance individual expressiveness with intermittent interaction at musically salient moments, such as phrase boundaries, synchronized rhythms, and call-and-response passages. Because these interactions are sparse and rhythm-dependent, existing audio-driven animation methods and conversational interaction models do not adequately capture their structure. Our key insight is that duet coordination can be represented as a time-varying signal that reflects how strongly performers engage with one another throughout a song. Based on this observation, we introduce interaction logits, an interpretable latent representation that models the degree of cross-performer engagement at each time step. We learn these logits using weak supervision and use them to condition an interaction-aware diffusion model jointly driven by audio features and interaction dynamics. This formulation enables unified multi-mode generation, spanning independent motion, coordinated behavior, and smooth transitions between them. Experiments show that InterSing generates realistic and expressive singing head animations with stronger coordination and musical alignment than existing methods, while preserving each performer's characteristic motion style. We further demonstrate that the same formulation generalizes to multi-singer performances and provides intuitive control over when and how performers engage.

</details>


### [45] [Methane Detection On Board Satellites from Unorthorectified Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04906)
*Luca Marini,Maggie Chen,Hala Lamdouar,Laura Martínez-Ferrer,Dr C. P. Bridges,Giacomo Acciarini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出UnorthoDOS，一个可直接用于未正射校正高光谱图像的机器学习训练数据集和方法，绕过传统正射校正和匹配滤波步骤。U-Net模型在未正射数据上接近正射数据的性能（IoU 16.91% vs 18.47%），均远超mag1c匹配滤波基线（IoU 4.76%），并展示了FP16压缩下模型尺寸减半、输出偏差<0.3%的星上部署可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 甲烷是强效温室气体和气候变化的主要驱动因素，及时检测对减排至关重要。传统检测方法依赖正射校正（orthorectification）修正几何畸变，以及匹配滤波器增强羽流信号，但这些步骤是为地面处理设计的，不适合星上实时执行。因此需要开发可直接在未正射校正的高光谱图像上训练的机器学习方法，以支持星上部署。

Method: 作者构建了UnorthoDOS数据集，包含来自EMIT传感器的正射和未正射高光谱图像。采用U-Net架构，直接在未正射数据上训练模型（跳过正射校正和匹配滤波），并与在正射数据上训练的模型及mag1c匹配滤波基线进行对比评估。此外通过FP16精度压缩测试星上部署可行性。

Result: 未正射数据训练的U-Net模型在全部羽流上的IoU达16.91%，正射数据训练的模型为18.47%，两者均大幅优于mag1c匹配滤波基线的4.76%。FP16压缩使模型尺寸减半，输出偏差低于0.3%，验证了星上部署的可行性。

Conclusion: 未正射校正的高光谱图像可直接用于机器学习甲烷羽流检测且性能接近正射数据，证明可绕过传统地面处理管线（正射校正和匹配滤波），为星上实时甲烷检测奠定了基础。数据集和代码已公开。

Abstract: As a potent greenhouse gas, methane is a major driver of climate change. Its effective mitigation relies on timely detection. Conventional detection methods rely on orthorectification to correct geometric distortions and matched filters to enhance plume signals, which are steps designed for ground processing and poorly suited to onboard execution. We introduce UnorthoDOS, a dataset and approach for training machine learning models directly on unorthorectified hyperspectral imagery, bypassing both orthorectification and matched-filter products. Our U-Net models trained on unorthorectified data approach the performance of models trained on orthorectified data (IoU 16.91% vs. 18.47% on all plumes), while both substantially outperform the mag1c matched-filter baseline (IoU 4.76%). We further demonstrate the feasibility of onboard deployment: FP16 compression halves model size with under 0.3% output deviation. The trained ML models and two ML-ready datasets -- orthorectified and unorthorectified hyperspectral imagery from the EMIT sensor -- are publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/SpaceML/UnorthoDOS, with code at https://github.com/spaceml-org/plume-hunter.

</details>


### [46] [TourPhysics: Bringing Physics to World Models for Exploration and Manipulation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04911)
*Xin Zhang,Yabo Chen,Zixuan Duan,Haibin Huang,Chi Zhang,Feng Xu,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TourPhysics是一个在线交互式视觉世界模型框架，从单张图像和声明式物理配置初始化，将确定性物理模拟与视频生成相结合，实现了持久化的场景探索与物体操作，在相机轨迹跟随、场景保持和长时程外观漂移抑制上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前视频世界模型主要依赖外观先验，难以在长时间跨度内保持物理一致性和空间一致性。此外，交互式视觉世界模型需要区分"观察"（相机运动揭示新表面）与"物理干预"（改变物体运动、接触和变形），但现有方法往往将两者混为一谈，导致长时程交互中物理或空间一致性丢失。

Method: TourPhysics是PhysOmni（ACM Multimedia 2026工作）的扩展，将有限物理驱动的视频合成为主扩展到持久探索与操控。其核心设计是：1) 确定性仿真与视频生成分离角色——仿真器负责计算有限物理与相机轨迹，生成器负责对应观测；2) 采纳的观测发布终端状态并更新外观记忆与后续生成器控制，已提交状态和仿真几何在合成与重试过程中保持不变；3) 将用于投影和可见性的仿真器几何与用于条件生成器的相对深度分离；4) 参考锚定记忆通过几何跨视角对应检索已接受的静态外观，并通过有界残差融入，无有效对应时回退至原生路径。

Result: 在仿真器定义的相机巡回和物体操控任务上，TourPhysics比评估的基线方法更紧密地遵循预设的相机和物体轨迹，保持输入场景不变，并在长时间跨度重访中减少外观漂移。

Conclusion: 通过对仿真状态、几何证据、生成器控制和外观记忆的明确角色分工，TourPhysics有效解决了视频世界模型在长时程交互中物理一致性与空间一致性不足的问题，为持久化的交互式世界建模提供了可行框架。

Abstract: Interactive visual world models must distinguish observation from physical intervention. Camera motion reveals new surfaces, whereas intervention changes object motion, contact, and deformation. Current video world models are largely driven by appearance priors and often lose physical or spatial consistency over long horizons. We present TourPhysics, an online framework initialized from a single image and a declarative physical configuration. TourPhysics extends PhysOmni, our ACM Multimedia 2026 work, from finite physics-grounded video synthesis to persistent exploration and manipulation. TourPhysics combines deterministic simulation with video generation while assigning separate roles to simulator state, geometric evidence, generator controls, and appearance memory. For each action, the simulator computes a finite physical and camera trajectory before the corresponding observation is generated. Accepted observations publish the terminal state and update the appearance memory and subsequent generator controls, while the committed state and simulator geometry remain fixed throughout synthesis and retry. We further separate the simulator geometry used for projection and visibility from the relative depth used to condition the generator. A reference-anchored memory retrieves accepted static appearance through geometric cross-view correspondence and incorporates it through a bounded residual that reverts to the native path when no valid correspondence exists. On simulator-defined camera tours and object manipulations, TourPhysics follows prescribed camera and object trajectories more closely than the evaluated baselines, preserves the input scene, and reduces appearance drift during long-horizon revisits.

</details>


### [47] [One Diffusion Model, Two Roles: Guided Trajectory Planning and Safety-Critical Scenario Generation in Closed-Loop Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04921)
*Arka Pal,Rajesh Kumar,Hannes Eriksson,Rémi Lacombe,Arvid Laveno Ling,Ankit Gupta,Maciej Wozniak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一的预训练扩散交通模型，可同时作为自车运动规划器和可控安全关键场景生成器用于压力测试规划器。规划侧提出SSDS扩散变换器解码器和DAPSE训练-free引导方案，生成侧利用同一模型在推理时生成长尾交互场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶开发中，运动规划与安全关键场景测试往往是分离的。标准基准测试难以覆盖长尾安全关键场景，且实际鲁棒性提升不够。作者希望用一个统一的交通先验模型同时服务规划与压力测试两个环节，揭示基准测试优越性与真实鲁棒性之间的差距。

Method: 1) SSDS (Single-Stream Dual-Stream) 扩散变换器解码器：通过联合注意力而非晚期交叉注意力融合场景上下文，提升闭环规划性能；2) DAPSE (Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling with Energy)：一种无需训练的引导方案，在干净样本层面注入任意能量函数，避免一阶近似误差且无需辅助网络；3) 同一扩散模型作为可控场景生成器，通过推理时引导使选定智能体趋向安全关键行为（激进切入、前车刹车、纵横向组合交互）。

Result: 在nuPlan闭环仿真中，SSDS规划器取得更强的名义性能；生成的安全关键场景暴露了标准基准下隐藏的失败模式，且SSDS规划器在这些挑战场景下退化更大，表明基准优越性不等同于鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 单一的预训练交通先验模型可同时改进运动规划并为系统化规划器鲁棒性评估提供现实框架，演示了统一交通模型在自动驾驶开发闭环中的双重价值。

Abstract: Diffusion probabilistic models can capture the multi-modal, interaction-rich distribution of joint future trajectories in driving scenes. We show that a single pretrained diffusion traffic model can serve two complementary roles in the autonomous driving development loop: as an ego motion planner, and as a controllable generator of safety-critical scenarios for stress-testing the planners. On the planning side, we introduce a Single-Stream Dual-Stream (SSDS) diffusion-transformer decoder that fuses scene context via joint attention rather than late cross-attention, improving closed-loop performance on nuPlan. We further propose Decoupled Annealing Posterior Sampling with Energy (DAPSE), a training-free guidance scheme that injects arbitrary energy functions at the clean-sample level, avoiding the first-order approximation errors while requiring no auxiliary networks. Beyond planning, we leverage the same diffusion model as a controllable scenario generator to create realistic long-tail driving interactions for closed-loop evaluation. Through inference-time guidance, selected agents are steered toward safety-critical behaviors, including aggressive cut-ins, lead-vehicle braking, and combined longitudinal-lateral interactions, while preserving realistic traffic behaviors. Evaluated in closed-loop nuPlan simulations with independent black-box planners, the generated scenarios expose failure modes that remain hidden under standard benchmarks. Although the SSDS-based planner achieves stronger nominal performance, it experiences larger degradation under these challenging scenarios, demonstrating that benchmark superiority does not necessarily translate to robustness. These results demonstrate that a single learned traffic prior can simultaneously improve motion planning and provide a realistic framework for systematic planner robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [48] [LensStyle: Learning the Optical Aesthetics for Controllable Stylized Lens Effect Rendering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04939)
*Yachuan Huang,Liwen Xiao,Liao Shen,Qiwen Wang,Huiqiang Sun,Zhiyu Pan,Zhiguo Cao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LensStyle，一个统一的可控风格化镜头效果渲染框架，通过联合连续-离散控制显式建模镜头美学。该模型采用双路径控制器（Dual-Path Controller），将连续光学参数调制（如对焦距离、模糊强度）与离散镜头风格条件（如圆形、多边形、甜甜圈、猫眼、星芒等效果）解耦，在单一框架内实现细粒度、可解释且基于物理的镜头操控。作者构建了MultiLens多镜头图像配对数据集用于训练，实验表明LensStyle在真实感、可控性和美学质量上优于现有镜头效果渲染方法和基于扩散的图像编辑模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有镜头效果渲染方法主要聚焦于从光圈小到大场景下模糊过渡的精确模拟，却忽略了镜头效果的美学风格方面，无法在光圈下生成多样化的散景效果，也无法捕捉小光圈下如星芒等独特的摄影现象。作者旨在弥合这一空白，提供一个能够显式建模镜头美学并支持多样化镜头风格渲染的统一框架。

Method: LensStyle框架包含一个双路径控制器（Dual-Path Controller），将连续光学参数调制（对焦距离、模糊强度）与离散镜头风格条件（圆形、多边形、甜甜圈、猫眼、星芒等散景效果）解耦，实现细粒度、可解释且物理可溯源的镜头操控。为支持训练，作者构建了MultiLens数据集，该数据集包含在真实光学约束下合成的多镜头图像配对样本。

Result: 大量实验表明，LensStyle在真实感、可控性和美学质量方面均优于现有镜头效果渲染方法以及基于扩散的图像编辑模型，有效推动了计算摄影向多样镜头风格模拟方向的发展。

Conclusion: LensStyle成功证明了通过联合连续-离散控制可以在单一统一框架内实现多样化、物理可解释的镜头效果渲染，拓展了计算摄影中镜头风格模拟的能力边界。

Abstract: The visual aesthetics of photographs are deeply influenced by lens characteristics such as aperture shape, optical vignetting and optical diffraction, which together define a camera's unique optical style. Existing lens effect rendering methods primarily focus on accurately simulating the blur transition from small to large apertures but overlook the stylistic aspects of lens effects. As a result, they fail to produce diverse bokeh effects under large apertures or capture distinctive photographic phenomena such as starbursts that emerge under small apertures. In this work, we introduce LensStyle, a unified framework for controllable stylized lens effect rendering that explicitly models lens aesthetics through joint continuous-discrete control. Our model incorporates a Dual-Path Controller that disentangles continuous optical parameter modulation (e.g., focus distance and blur strength) from discrete lens-style conditioning (e.g., circular, polygonal, donut, cat-eye, and starburst effects), enabling fine-grained, interpretable, and physically grounded lens manipulation within a single unified framework. To support model training, we curate a comprehensive MultiLens dataset containing multi-lens image pairs synthesized under real optical constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LensStyle achieves superior realism, controllability, and aesthetic quality compared with existing lens effect rendering approaches and diffusion-based image editing models, advancing computational photography toward multiple-lens-style simulation.

</details>


### [49] [Learning 3D Editing without Paired Supervision via Generative Prior Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04942)
*Hao Wen,Weibin Yun,Hongxing Fan,Haotian Lu,Rui Chen,Zehuan Huang,Lu Sheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PriorEdit3D框架，通过生成先验蒸馏（Generative Prior Distillation）实现无需3D配对监督的前馈式3D编辑。方法从基础模型中蒸馏视觉、语义和几何知识到3D编辑模型，利用2D视觉先验和VLM语义先验进行监督，并引入3D感知分布匹配正则化约束几何合理性，显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指令引导的3D编辑对交互式内容创作至关重要，但高质量配对训练数据的严重匮乏是根本瓶颈。现有方法要么依赖缓慢的测试时优化，要么通过复杂管道构造伪配对数据训练，后者常引入结构漂移和几何伪影。

Method: 核心思想是从强大的基础模型中蒸馏视觉、语义和几何知识到3D编辑模型，而非依赖真实3D配对。具体包含三个关键组件：(1) 通过可微渲染管道，在主编辑视角用2D图像编辑模型的视觉先验进行监督；(2) 在新视角用视觉-语言模型的语义先验确保严格指令遵循和源身份保持；(3) 引入3D感知分布匹配正则化，在3D隐空间中约束编辑输出保持在预训练图像到3D教师模型定义的逼真3D资产流形内，以解决几何塌缩和多视角不一致问题。

Result: 大量实验表明该方法在指令遵循度和跨视角一致性上达到优越表现，显著优于现有最先进基线。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过生成先验蒸馏可以在无需3D配对监督的情况下训练高质量的3D编辑模型，为解决训练数据稀缺瓶颈提供了有效路径。

Abstract: Instruction-guided 3D editing is essential for interactive content creation, yet it faces a significant bottleneck: the severe scarcity of high-quality paired training data. Existing approaches attempt to bypass this by either relying on slow test-time optimization or training on pseudo-pairs constructed via complex pipelines, which often introduce structural drift and geometric artifacts. In this paper, we propose a novel framework that learns feed-forward 3D editing without paired 3D supervision via Generative Prior Distillation. Instead of relying on ground-truth 3D pairs, our core idea is to distill visual, semantic, and geometric knowledge from powerful foundation models directly into a 3D editing model. Specifically, through a differentiable rendering pipeline, we supervise the 3D representation using two complementary signals: a 2D visual prior from an image editing model at the main editing view, and a semantic prior from a Vision-Language Model at novel views to ensure strict instruction following and source identity preservation. Crucially, to address the geometric collapse and multi-view inconsistencies inherent in 2D projection supervision, we introduce a 3D-aware Distribution Matching regularization. Acting as a geometric prior, this term operates in the 3D latent space, constraining the edited output to remain within the manifold of realistic 3D assets defined by a pretrained image to 3D teacher model. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our method achieves superior instruction fidelity and cross-view consistency, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art baselines. Our project is available at: https://github.com/thiamine128/PriorEdit3D.

</details>


### [50] [MCPO: Modality-Contrastive Preference Optimization for Multimodal Chain-of-Thought Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04947)
*Guangheng Yang,Zhenliang Ni,Zhenkai Wu,Han Shu,Juan Feng,Wenming Yang,Jie Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MCPO（Modality-Contrastive Preference Optimization）的高样本效率两阶段长度压缩方法，用于压缩多模态大规模推理模型的长思维链（M-CoT）。该方法仅需不到900个训练样本，通过跨模态互信息（NCMI）剪枝和不对称多模态长度控制偏好优化，可将推理链长度缩短高达69.5%，实现最高3.34倍的端到端推理加速，同时保持原有精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大规模推理模型通过长思维链（M-CoT）解决复杂任务时表现优越，但过长的推理轨迹带来巨大计算成本和显著的KV-cache压力。现有CoT压缩与对齐范式依赖静态规则或单一维度偏好，缺乏细粒度的跨模态约束，容易引发"视觉惰性"（visual laziness）和幻觉式推理（hallucinatory reasoning）问题。

Method: MCPO采用两阶段设计：（1）压缩阶段：提出步骤级归一化跨模态互信息（NCMI）剪枝算法，通过比较有图与无图上下文下的推理差异，自动识别并移除视觉无关的推理步骤，减少冗余和幻觉内容；（2）对齐阶段：先进行监督微调实现领域自适应初始化，再利用不对称多模态长度控制偏好损失优化，该损失采用高度非线性的比值比（odds-ratio）公式，在有图上下文中提供陡峭梯度以强化偏好轨迹的长度约束，在无图上下文中使用缩放且平坦的线性差值以维持模态一致性，从而实现稳定的跨模态偏好对齐。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL-Thinking等主流基座模型上的大量实验表明，MCPO可将CoT长度缩短最多69.5%，实现高达3.34倍的端到端推理加速，同时保持原始任务精度。

Conclusion: MCPO通过引入跨模态对比的细粒度约束，有效解决了长思维链推理中大计算开销和KV-cache压力问题，同时避免了现有压缩方法中因缺乏模态感知而导致的视觉惰性和幻觉推理。该方法具有高样本效率（<900训练样本），为多模态推理模型的长度压缩提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Recently, multimodal large-scale reasoning models have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in solving complex tasks through long Chains-of-Thought (M-CoT). However, excessively long reasoning trajectories incur substantial computational costs and significant KV-cache pressure. Existing CoT compression and alignment paradigms mainly rely on static rules or single-dimensional preferences, lacking fine-grained cross-modal constraints; as a result, they are prone to inducing visual laziness and hallucinatory reasoning. To address these issues, we propose Modality-Contrastive Preference Optimization (MCPO), a highly sample-efficient two-stage length-compression method that requires fewer than 900 training samples. In the compression stage, we introduce a step-level Normalized Cross-Modal Mutual Information (NCMI) pruning algorithm, which automatically identifies and removes visual-independent reasoning steps by comparing the reasoning discrepancies between with-image and no-image contexts. This significantly reduces redundancy and hallucinatory content in the reasoning chains. In the alignment stage, the model first undergoes supervised fine-tuning to achieve domain-adaptive initialization, followed by optimization using an asymmetric multimodal length-controlled preference loss. This objective adopts a highly nonlinear odds-ratio formulation that provides steep gradients in the with-image context to reinforce length constraints for preferred trajectories, while applying a scaled, flat-gradient linear difference in the no-image context to maintain modality consistency, thereby achieving stable cross-modal preference alignment. Extensive experiments on mainstream base models such as Qwen3-VL-Thinking show that our method can reduce CoT length by up to 69.5% and achieve up to 3.34x end-to-end inference speedup while preserving original accuracy.

</details>


### [51] [VICAL: Vicinal Consistency Alignment for Long-Tailed Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04948)
*Jiangang Zhu,Zheng Wang,Bin Zhu,Yi-Ping Phoebe Chen,Jingjing Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VICAL框架，挑战了长尾学习中多专家模型依赖专家多样性的主流假设，主张通过降低预测方差（而非最大化多样性）来提升长尾识别性能。VICAL包含两个核心组件：自一致性学习（平滑局部损失景观、缓解过拟合）和深度集成蒸馏（通过低分辨率视图促进跨专家的低频语义一致性），在CIFAR-LT、ImageNet-LT和iNaturalist 2018上均取得了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多专家模型已成为长尾学习的主流范式，其成功通常被归因于专家多样性的提升。然而作者重新审视了这一核心假设，发现通过logit调整或显式正则化引入的多样性并不保证更好的集成准确率。作者提出多专家模型的增益更多来自方差缩减而非多样性最大化，从而质疑并修正了现有研究的理论基础。

Method: VICAL框架包含两个关键组件：(1) Self-Consistency Learning（自一致性学习）：抑制模型对不稳定的高频信息的依赖，平滑局部损失景观，从而缓解过拟合并提升对尾部类别的泛化能力；(2) Deep Ensemble Distillation（深度集成蒸馏）：利用低分辨率视图促进跨专家间的低频语义一致性，从而避免与已有知识的优化冲突。

Result: 在CIFAR-LT、ImageNet-LT和iNaturalist 2018三个长尾基准数据集上的大量实验表明，VICAL一致性地优于现有最先进方法，验证了基于一致性设计（方差缩减导向）的有效性。

Conclusion: 作者通过实证和理论论证推翻了"多样性最大化必有益"的假设，指出多专家模型在长尾学习中的成功关键在于预测方差的降低。VICAL从一致性角度出发，为长尾识别提供了一种新的设计范式，代码已开源。

Abstract: Multi-expert models have become the dominant paradigm for long-tailed learning, largely attributed to their presumed ability to benefit from expert diversity. However, we revisit this central assumption and reveal that diversity induced by logit adjustment or explicit regularizers does not guarantee better ensemble accuracy. Our work suggests that multi-expert models benefit more from variance reduction than diversity maximization. We introduce \textbf{VICAL}, a \textbf{VI}cinal \textbf{C}onsistency \textbf{AL}ignment framework that improves long-tailed recognition not by enforcing expert diversity, but by reducing prediction variance. Specifically, our approach comprises two key components: Self-Consistency Learning and Deep Ensemble Distillation. Self-Consistency Learning discourages reliance on unstable high-frequency information, smoothing the local loss landscape and mitigating overfitting, especially for tail classes. Deep Ensemble Distillation promotes cross-expert low-frequency semantic agreement using a low-resolution view, thereby sidestepping optimization conflicts with established knowledge. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-LT, ImageNet-LT, and iNaturalist 2018 show that VICAL consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, validating the effectiveness of our consistency-driven design. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/FlamieZhu/Vicinal-Consistency-Alignment}{VICAL}.

</details>


### [52] [MINT: A Unified Model for World-Space Camera and Hand Motion Estimation from Scalable Egocentric Pipeline Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04958)
*Zijie Zhu,Weiren Cai,Yizhou Wang,Zhenjie Yang,Yide Liu,Jiahao Chen,Guanqi He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MINT是首个直接从第一人称RGB视频中联合预测世界坐标系下相机轨迹和双手轨迹的基础模型，通过共享时空视频表征同时预测相机运动、手部状态和手部存在概率，然后经坐标变换输出世界空间手部运动。为解决标注稀缺问题，作者开发了开源的EGOPIPELINE标注流程，将大规模公开第一人称视频转化为结构化的双手轨迹监督数据，采用先在大规模伪标签上预训练再在小规模高质量联合标注上微调的策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的第一人称视频中相机与手部运动恢复系统通常将问题分解为相机运动、深度估计、手部重建和轨迹细化等多个独立阶段，带来大量计算开销，且无法对相机和手部运动进行联合建模。此外，世界空间下的成对相机和手部标注数据稀缺，限制了大规模模型训练。

Method: MINT从单一共享的时空视频表征出发，联合预测相机轨迹、相机坐标系下的手部状态以及逐帧手部存在概率，随后通过显式坐标变换生成世界空间下的手部运动。训练采用两阶段策略：先在作者开发的EGOPIPELINE生成的约1,021小时大规模伪标签数据上预训练，再在小规模高质量联合标注上进行微调。作者还公开了标注流程和训练推理代码。

Result: 在多个公开基准上，MINT在世界空间手部轨迹精度上有显著提升，相机轨迹估计也得到改进，端到端轨迹生成速度相比标注流程加快，且能零样本泛化到未见过的第一人称数据集。

Conclusion: MINT验证了通过共享视频表征联合建模相机与手部运动的高效性与可行性，提供了一个涵盖模型、代码、标注流程及1,021小时数据集的全套开源方案，为该方向后续研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Recovering camera and hand motion in world coordinates from egocentric video is a key capability for activity understanding, robot learning, and augmented reality. Existing systems typically decompose this problem into separate stages for camera motion, depth, hand reconstruction, and trajectory refinement, resulting in substantial computational overhead and preventing the joint modeling of camera and hand motion. We introduce MINT (Minting IN-the-Wild Trajectories), the first foundation model that directly produces complete world-space two-hand trajectories from ego-centric RGB video. From a single shared spatiotemporal video representation, MINT jointly predicts the camera trajectory, camera-frame hand states, and per-frame hand presence, and then produces world-space hand motion via explicit coordinate transformations. Training such a model at scale is challenging, since paired world-space camera and hand annotations are scarce. We therefore develop an open-source labeling EGOPIPELINE that converts large collections of public egocentric videos into structured camera-and-hand trajectory supervision. MINT is first pretrained on these large-scale pseudo-labels and then fine-tuned on a small set of high-quality joint annotations. Across public benchmarks, MINT achieves [xxx] improvement in world-space hand trajectory accuracy, [xxx] improvement in camera trajectory estimation, and [xxx] faster end-to-end trajectory generation than the labeling pipeline, while generalizing zero-shot to unseen egocentric datasets. We release the model, training and inference code, labeling pipeline, and a curated 1,021-hour egocentric trajectory dataset.

</details>


### [53] [ARC-Loc: Leveraging Azimuthal Ray Convergence as a Geometric Cue for Direct Cross-View Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04965)
*Hyeongsik Kim,Mincheol Kim,Heejoon Moon,Je Hyeong Hong*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为ARC-Loc的新型跨视角定位方法，通过模仿人类的"后方交会"导航技术，将地面图像关键点转换为卫星地图上的方位角射线并行交点来确定位置，无需依赖BEV变换或外部深度基础模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的跨视角定位方法依赖BEV变换或2D-to-3D提升来弥合视角差异，但单个地面图像推导3D结构本质上是病态问题，导致几何畸变和计算开销。依赖外部深度基础模型会引入延迟且易受噪声预测影响。

Method: 该方法的核心洞察是：(i) 地面关键点可转换为卫星地图上的方位角射线；(ii) 这些射线理想情况下汇聚于用户位置。利用这一几何约束，提出了最小方位角射线汇聚(ARC)求解器来寻找交点，以及ARC损失函数来优化匹配网络。

Result: 在VIGOR和KITTI数据集上的实验表明，ARC-Loc在保持与近期方法相当定位精度的同时，实现了更快、更节省内存的推理，且与现有框架具有兼容性。

Conclusion: 通过消除对计算密集的BEV变换和外部深度基础模型的依赖，ARC-Loc提供了更高效实用的跨视图定位方案，验证了基于方位角射线汇聚的显式特征匹配思路的有效性。

Abstract: Cross-view localization (CVL) estimates the pose of a ground image by matching it to a geo-referenced satellite image. To bridge the extreme viewpoint gap, mainstream pipelines rely on Bird's-Eye-View (BEV) transformations or 2D-to-3D lifting. However, deriving 3D structures from a single ground image is fundamentally ill-posed, causing these methods to endure geometric distortions and computational costs during 3D lifting or BEV projection. Furthermore, relying on external depth foundation models to resolve this introduces latency and remains susceptible to noisy predictions. In this work, we present a different approach inspired by a human navigation technique called resection, that can perform direct ground to satellite image matching and localization without relying on external depth foundation models. The key insights of our method are that (i) ground keypoints can be translated into azimuthal rays on the satellite map, and (ii) these rays ideally converge at the user location. Exploiting this geometric constraint through direct line-to-point correspondences, we introduce a minimal Azimuthal Ray Convergence (ARC) solver to identify the intersection, alongside an ARC loss to optimize the matching network. By eliminating dependencies on computationally heavy BEV transformations and external depth foundation models, our approach achieves faster, memory-efficient inference, while its explicit feature matching ensures straightforward compatibility with existing frameworks. Experiments on VIGOR and KITTI demonstrate that ARC-Loc maintains competitive localization accuracy compared to recent approaches, highlighting its practicality.

</details>


### [54] [RefDiT: Local Attribute Guidance in Reference-Based Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04976)
*Rameshwar Mishra,Srikrishna Karanam,A V Subramanyam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RefDiT提出了一种基于扩散Transformer的参考图像引导生成框架，通过属性级分解标识符令牌和局部区域引导，解决现有方法在复杂场景中无法对多对象局部属性进行精确控制的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化生成和风格迁移方法在参考图像仅含单一对象时表现良好，能捕获包含所有隐含属性的全局风格。然而在包含多个具有不同属性特征的对象的复杂真实场景中，这类依赖全局级引导的方法无法定位参考图像中的相关局部元素，导致无法基于局部属性生成新图像。此外，现有方法通常使用单一标识符令牌捕获参考图像的所有细节，缺乏单个属性级别的控制能力。

Method: RefDiT框架的输入包括参考图像、文本提示和可选的用户引导上下文。其核心方法包括：(1) 利用局部元素的属性进行局部区域引导；(2) 通过对标识符令牌进行属性级分解，从参考图像构建属性感知的条件信号；(3) 在推理提示中进行上下文调整，用于训练扩散Transformer（DiT）生成模型的低秩适配器（LoRA）模块。RefDiT学习标识符令牌与参考图像中局部区域之间的对应关系，从而实现更有效的局部引导。

Result: 文章未在摘要中给出具体的定量或定性实验结果。

Conclusion: RefDiT通过学习标识符令牌与参考图像局部区域之间的对应关系，在复杂真实场景中实现了更有效的局部属性级别引导，克服了全局级引导方法的局限。

Abstract: Personalization models generate new images guided by a few subject references, while style transfer methods aim to produce images aligned with a global style derived from a reference image. Recent approaches perform well when the reference image contains a single object, effectively capturing a global style that encompasses all implicit attributes. However, when applied to complex real-world scenes containing multiple objects with distinct attribute characteristics, these methods, due to their global-level guidance, fail to localize relevant elements in the reference image. The global guidance restricts their ability to generate new images based on the local attributes in the reference image. Moreover, existing methods typically employ a single identifier token to capture all details from the reference, resulting in a lack of individual, attribute-level control. Motivated by these limitations, we propose RefDiT, a novel framework for reference-guided image generation. RefDiT takes as input a reference image, a text prompt, and an optional user-provided guidance context. RefDiT employs local region guidance using the attributes of local elements. It constructs an attribute-aware conditioning signal from the reference image by performing attribute-level decomposition of the identifier token and performs context adjustment in the inference prompt to train low-rank adapter (LoRA) blocks of a diffusion transformer (DiT)-based generative model. RefDiT learns the correspondence between identifier tokens and local regions in the reference image, enabling more effective local guidance.

</details>


### [55] [Temporal Residual Neural Radiance Fields for Monocular Video Dynamic Human Body Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04984)
*Tianle Du,Jie Wang,Xiaolong Xie,Wei Li,Pengxiang Su,Jie Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时间残差神经辐射场（Temporal Residual Neural Radiance Fields）的动态场景人体三维建模方法，通过时间残差场表示、参数精简与多维损失函数设计，在保持与Anim-NeRF和Neural Body相当精度的同时，实现了近780倍的训练时间效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于MLP的方法虽然在静态场景人体重建中取得较好效果，但MLP存在容量限制，在处理动态场景时需大量训练时间和计算资源，且重建质量受限。因此需要一种能高效处理复杂时空信号的动态场景人体三维建模方法。

Method: 1)构建与MLP架构无关的时间残差场以处理视频序列中的时间信号表示问题；2)提出集成化方法减少可训练参数并加速渲染，增强网络特征表示能力；3)设计多维损失函数以精确度量预测与实际空间像素值之间的损失。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在PSNR和SSIM等精度指标上优于最新的代表性方法，并与Anim-NeRF和Neural Body保持相似精度，同时实现了近780倍的训练时间效率提升。

Conclusion: 提出的时间残差神经辐射场方法能有效平衡动态场景人体重建的精度与效率，在保持高精度重建的同时大幅提升训练效率，为动态场景人体建模提供了高效的解决方案。

Abstract: In the field of computer vision and graphics, high-quality reconstruction of the human body in static scenes has been achieved in recent years by a single multilayer perceptron (MLP) in a number of approaches. However, MLPs have capacity limitations, requiring substantial training time and computational resources for dynamic scene reconstruction. And the quality of reconstruction is significantly constrained. This paper proposes a method for effectively processing complex spatiotemporal signals in dynamic scene human 3D modeling. The proposed method uses Temporal Residual Neural Radiance Fields to achieve novel view rendering and new pose synthesis of human bodies.To address the problem of representing temporal signals in video sequences, we construct a temporal residual field which is not related to the MLP architecture. Secondly, to improve reconstruction efficiency, we propose an integrated approach that reduces trainable parameters and accelerates rendering, thereby enhancing the network's feature representation capability. Finally, we design a multi-dimensional loss function to accurately measure the loss between predicted and actual spatial pixel values. The experimental results show that our proposed approach improves the peak signal-to-noise ratio (PSNR) and structural similarity index (SSIM) accuracy metrics compared to the latest representative methods. It maintains similar accuracy to Anim-NeRF and Neural Body while achieving a nearly 780-fold increase in time efficiency.

</details>


### [56] [Compositional Reward Models for Conditional Medical Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05028)
*Aayush Kumar Tyagi,Prathosh A. P.,Mausam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PRISM 框架旨在解决条件医学图像生成中现有扩散模型无法捕捉细粒度属性和语义一致性的问题。它通过将图像质量分解为多个可验证的阶段奖励（从粗到细），并采用分层约束传播（HCP）机制确保细粒度正确性优先于粗粒度奖励，从而提升生成质量。在PanNuke、CeDeM和ISIC三个数据集上验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学图像标注昂贵且耗时，条件扩散模型（如ControlNet）虽可基于语义掩码和文本生成图像，但现有方法无法捕捉细粒度属性（如强度和纹理）和领域专家期望的语义一致性。强化学习微调的现有尝试因依赖单一标量奖励会混淆多种失败模式，提供弱纠错信号。

Method: 提出PRISM框架，一种组合奖励模型（CRM）方法。将图像质量分解为可验证的分阶段维度，每个阶段评估不同层次的正确性：低层属性（强度与纹理）、与条件输入的结构对齐、高层语义保真度。通过分层约束传播（HCP）机制组合阶段奖励，强制执行从细到粗的正确性概念，确保低层缺陷先被解决，防止简单目标掩盖关键失败。

Result: 在三个涵盖多样医学影像任务的数据集上评估：PanNuke（mDice提升2.3%）、CeDeM（MRE降低8.5%）、ISIC（F1提升5.9%），均优于最接近的基线方法。

Conclusion: PRISM通过分解多维度奖励并采用分层约束传播机制，有效解决了条件医学图像生成中单一奖励信号导致的失败模式混淆问题，能够生成更符合领域专家期望的图像，提升下游任务的性能。

Abstract: Acquiring high quality annotated medical image data is critical for training deep learning models; however, annotation is expensive, time consuming, and requires domain expertise. Conditional diffusion models, such as ControlNet, offer an alternative by generating images conditioned on semantic masks and text. However, existing approaches fail to capture fine grained properties (e.g., intensity and texture), as well as semantic consistency expected by domain experts, limiting their effectiveness for downstream tasks. Recent attempts to address these issues using reinforcement learning fine-tuning remain limited due to the reliance on a single scalar reward, which conflates diverse failure modes and provides weak corrective signals. We propose PRISM, a Compositional Reward Model (CRM) framework for conditional medical image generation. Instead of assigning a single reward, we decompose image quality into verifier grounded stages, each evaluating a distinct aspect of correctness from fine to coarse properties, including low level attributes (intensity and texture), structural alignment with conditioning inputs, and high level semantic fidelity. These stage wise rewards are composed through a Hierarchical Constrained Propagation (HCP) mechanism that enforces a fine to coarse notion of correctness, ensuring that lower level deficiencies are resolved before higher level rewards are accrued, preventing easier objectives from masking critical failures.
  We evaluate PRISM across three datasets spanning diverse medical imaging tasks: PanNuke (multi-class cell segmentation), CeDeM (villi/crypt detection and measurement), and ISIC (skin lesion classification). Training downstream models with data generated by PRISM yields improvements over closest baselines, including a 2.3% increase in mDice on PanNuke, a 8.5% reduction in Mean Relative Error (MRE) on CeDeM, and increases ISIC F1 by 5.9%.

</details>


### [57] [PuTR-CouT: Counting-by-Tracking in Camera-Trap Image Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05038)
*Fagner Cunha,Juan G. Colonna,Eulanda M. dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PuTR-CouT框架，利用基于Transformer的学习关联机制对相机陷阱图像进行序列级动物计数（counting-by-tracking），通过合成伪跟踪标签解决标注稀缺问题，并改进了MaxBoxCount基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱图像中的物种识别已被广泛研究，但生态建模任务（如物种丰度或密度估计）还需要对个体动物进行计数。然而，大多数数据集缺乏计数标签，且低帧率（约1帧/秒）使序列级跟踪和计数估算极具挑战性。

Method: 提出PuTR-CouT计数-跟踪框架：1）利用静态背景和短期时间突发等结构先验，以弱监督方式启发式生成伪跟踪标签合成训练数据；2）基于Transformer的学习关联机制构建跟踪器，跨帧关联检测结果；3）利用轨迹估计每物种计数；4）改进iWildCam 2021挑战赛中顶级方案使用的MaxBoxCount启发式方法作为强基线。

Result: 在iWildCam 2021基准上，PuTR-CouT相比改进后的MaxBoxCount取得了具有竞争力的计数结果，同时具备多物种预测和轨迹级验证的能力；改进的MaxBoxCount还创下了迄今报告的最高得分。

Conclusion: PuTR-CouT通过counting-by-tracking范式有效解决了低帧率相机陷阱图像中缺乏计标注的个体计数问题，并能在保持竞争力的同时提供多物种预测与轨迹验证等额外功能。

Abstract: Species identification in camera trap images has been widely studied, but key ecological modeling tasks such as species abundance or density estimation also require counting individual animals. However, the lack of counting labels in most datasets and low frame rates (typically ~1 frame per second) make sequence-level tracking and count estimation particularly challenging. In this work, we present PuTR-CouT, a counting-by-tracking framework built on a transformer-based learned association mechanism for sequence-level animal counting in camera trap images. To address the scarcity of annotated tracking data, we generate synthetic training data by exploiting structural priors, such as static backgrounds and short temporal bursts, to heuristically create pseudo-tracking labels in a weakly supervised manner. The resulting tracker associates detections across frames, using these tracks to estimate per-species counts. We also refine the MaxBoxCount heuristic used by the top solutions of the iWildCam 2021 challenge as a strong baseline, setting the highest score reported to date. When evaluated on the iWildCam 2021 benchmark, our framework PuTR-CouT delivers competitive counting results compared to the improved MaxBoxCount, with the added capability of multi-species predictions and track-level verification.

</details>


### [58] [Efficient Multi-Timescale Event Representations for Feed-Forward Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05049)
*Fredrik Lundell,Per-Erik Forssen,Mårten Wadenbäck,Astrid Lundmark*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于置信度归一化连续多时间尺度表示的神经形态目标检测方法，利用对数B样条时间编码和几何感知局部置信度机制直接在事件表示中编码时间信息，使前馈检测器在PEDRo和Gen1数据集上优于紧凑CSTR表示，并引入递归指数-多项式近似实现逐事件高效更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机目标检测通常依赖递归架构来累积稀疏时间信息，但这种架构延迟高、计算开销大。作者希望探究是否可以将时间信息直接编码到事件表示中，从而让前馈网络也能捕获时间信息，为高效的事件驱动和神经形态目标检测奠定基础。

Method: 提出置信度归一化的连续多时间尺度事件表示，核心包括：(1) 基于对数B样条的时间编码以非线性方式聚合时序信息；(2) 利用事件生成空间结构的几何感知局部置信度机制；(3) 引入递归指数-多项式近似，支持逐事件（event-by-event）高效更新而不牺牲检测精度。

Result: 使用固定的前馈EventCenterNet检测器，所提出的表示方法在PEDRo和Gen1数据集上一致优于紧凑CSTR表示；递归指数-多项式近似在保持大部分检测性能的同时实现了高效逐事件更新。

Conclusion: 精心设计的事件表示可以捕获大部分原本依赖递归时间建模才能学到的时间信息，说明前馈、事件驱动及神经形态检测具有广阔前景。

Abstract: Autonomous systems require robust low-latency perception under rapidly changing scene dynamics and challenging illumination. In event cameras object detection commonly relies on recurrent architectures to accumulate sparse temporal information over time. This work investigates how temporal information can be encoded directly within the event representation. We propose a confidence-normalized continuous multi-timescale representation based on logarithmic B-spline temporal encoding together with a geometry-aware local confidence mechanism that exploits the spatial structure of event generation. Using a fixed feed-forward EventCenterNet detector, we show that the proposed representations consistently outperform the compact CSTR representation on PEDRo and Gen1 datasets. We further introduce a recursive exponential-polynomial approximation that enables efficient event-by-event updates while largely preserving detection performance. These results demonstrate that carefully designed event representations can capture a substantial portion of the temporal information learned through recurrent temporal modeling, providing a promising foundation for efficient feed-forward, event-driven, and future neuromorphic object detection.

</details>


### [59] [Adaptive Multi-Granularity Temporal Modeling for Weakly Supervised Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05066)
*Changyi Li,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应的时序建模框架用于弱监督视频异常检测（WSVAD），通过时间细化模块（TRM）和自适应事件分割模块（ESM）分别建模长程时间依赖和可变时长/频率的异常事件，并采用基于相似度的自适应融合策略替代固定top-k聚合方法，在两个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大多数WSVAD方法基于多示例学习（MIL）框架，依赖刚性的手工时序先验来监督异常打分，难以适应真实视频中异常时长与时间动态的广泛变化，导致片段级预测不稳定或不可靠。

Method: 1) 提出时间细化模块（TRM）：利用动态位置编码与可学习类令牌建模长程时序依赖，蒸馏稳定全局视频级表示；2) 提出自适应事件分割模块（ESM）：通过时序不连续分析识别事件边界，将片段特征聚合成判别性事件级表示，以捕捉不同频率与时长的异常事件；3) 提出自适应相似度融合策略：动态整合片段级与事件级异常分数为视频级预测，以全局语义相关性替代固定top-k聚合启发式。

Result: 在两个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，所提框架持续优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过在多时间粒度上显式建模视频动态变化，该自适应时序建模框架有效解决了MIL框架在WSVAD中对异常时长和动态变化适应性不足的问题，为弱监督视频异常检测提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: As the scale of video surveillance data outpaces manual annotation capacities, weakly supervised video anomaly detection (WSVAD) has emerged as a critical research frontier. Most existing approaches formulate WSVAD within a Multiple Instance Learning (MIL) framework that relies on rigid, hand-crafted temporal priors to supervise anomaly scoring. However, such formulations exhibit limited adaptability to the wide variation in anomaly durations and temporal dynamics observed in real-world videos, often leading to unstable or unreliable snippet-level predictions. To address this limitation, we propose an adaptive temporal modeling framework for WSVAD that explicitly accounts for variations in video dynamics across multiple temporal granularities. First, we introduce a Temporal Refinement Module (TRM) that leverages dynamic positional encoding and a learnable class token to model long-range temporal dependencies while distilling a stable global video-level representation. Second, to capture anomalous events with varying frequency and duration, we develop an adaptive Event Segmentation Module (ESM) that identifies event boundaries through temporal discontinuity analysis and aggregates snippet features into discriminative event-level representations. Finally, for snippet-level and event-level predictions, we propose an adaptive similarity-based fusion strategy that dynamically integrates anomaly scores into video-level predictions, replacing fixed top-k aggregation heuristics with global semantic relevance. Extensive experiments on two benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [60] [MultiAttenGastro: Multi-Dimensional Attention Augmentation for Gastrointestinal Endoscopy Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05070)
*Sadhana Devarajan,Praveen Kumar Chandaliya,Dhruvin Jashvant Kumar Shah,Kishor Upla,Kiran Raja*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MultiAttenGastro，一种包含并行1-D通道、2-D空间和3-D上下文头的即插即用注意力框架，并在五个公开胃肠道（GI）内镜数据集和八个CNN/Transformer骨干网络上进行了首次系统性跨数据集评估。发现注意力有效性并非普遍适用，而是与ImageNet预训练特征和目标分布之间的表征差距相关：在差距大的数据集上有增益，差距小时（Kvasir-v2）全部负收益，中等差距时结果混合。进一步通过CKA分析表明多头注意力的组合增益与表征冗余度相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胃肠道内镜自动分类需要模型在不同模态和类别分布间泛化，这些分布通常与自然图像预训练相距甚远。现有注意力设计缺乏系统性的跨骨干和跨数据集验证，且对不同场景下注意力是否有效、何时有效缺乏理论理解。

Method: 提出MultiAttenGastro即插即用注意力框架，包含三个并行分支：1-D通道注意力头、2-D空间注意力头和3-D上下文注意力头。在五个公开GI数据集（含Kvasir-Capsule和Kvasir-v2）和八个CNN及Transformer骨干网络上进行80次骨干-数据集组合的系统评估。使用五个种子的消融实验和配对t检验/Wilcoxon检验，并采用CKA（中心核对齐）分析表征冗余度与注意力有效性之间的关系。

Result: 在Kvasir-Capsule（14类WCE）上，MultiAttenGastro在8个骨干中改善6个，最佳宏F1达98.33%；在表征差距小的Kvasir-v2基准上8个骨干全部负增益；中间差距数据集上结果混合。最强案例（Kvasir-Capsule + ConvNeXt-Tiny）的五种子消融表明改进方向一致但统计上不显著（配对t检验p=0.47，Wilcoxon p=0.63），个体注意力头单独使用时并非均有益，只有组合才产生正向平均效果。CKA分析显示：大领域差距下低inter-head CKA（低表征冗余）与一致性增益吻合，小差距下高冗余与损失吻合。

Conclusion: 多维注意力的有效性取决于ImageNet特征与目标GI分布之间的表征差距：差距越大，注意力越可能产生增益。该工作定位为提供何时及为何多维注意力有助于GI内镜分类的证据性分析，而非主张MultiAttenGastro是严格更优的架构选择。值得注意的是即使在最强用例上改进也未达到统计显著性，反映真实医疗影像场景中架构改进的效应量通常有限。

Abstract: Automated gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopy classification requires models that generalize across diverse modalities and class distributions, often far from natural-image pretraining. We propose MultiAttenGastro, a plug-and-play attention framework with parallel 1-D channel, 2-D spatial, and 3-D contextual heads, and present the first systematic cross-dataset evaluation across eight CNN and transformer backbones on five public GI datasets (80 backbone--dataset runs). We find that attention effectiveness is not universal but tracks the representational gap between ImageNet features and the target distribution: MultiAttenGastro improves 6 of 8 backbones on Kvasir-Capsule (14-class WCE, large gap; best macro F1 98.33\%), is uniformly negative on the small-gap Kvasir-v2 benchmark (0/8), and shows mixed outcomes on datasets with intermediate gap. Five-seed ablation on the strongest case (Kvasir-Capsule, ConvNeXt-Tiny) shows this improvement is directionally consistent, but not statistically decisive (paired $t$: $p=0.47$; Wilcoxon: $p=0.63$), and that individual attention heads are not uniformly beneficial in isolation only their combination yields a positive mean effect. Centered Kernel Alignment (CKA) analysis links this pattern to representational redundancy: low inter-head CKA under large domain gaps coincides with the framework's only consistent gains, while high redundancy under small gaps coincides with its losses. We report these results, including the non-significant margins, as evidence for when and why multi-dimensional attention helps GI endoscopy classification, rather than as a claim that MultiAttenGastro is a strictly superior architectural choice.

</details>


### [61] [Training-Free Logical and Structural Anomaly Detection via Calibrated Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05091)
*Changyi Li,Miao Yu,Kai Dong,Yu Xiao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需训练的工业异常检测方法，通过正常集校准将计数能力引入训练无关框架，同时处理逻辑异常（违反全局规则）和结构异常（局部纹理损坏）。在MVTec-LOCO上取得逻辑异常89.0和结构异常95.9的图像级AUROC，平均92.5，为训练无关检测器中的最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测需要同时处理两类缺陷：结构异常（局部纹理损坏）和逻辑异常（违反对象数量、组成或排列等全局规则）。现有检测器通常偏向其中一类：训练无关方法有效利用冻结表示但缺乏显式的对象计数能力，而能推理计数的方法通常依赖类别特定的组件建模，两者难以兼顾。

Method: 提出正常集校准（normal-set calibration）技术，利用正常图像的统计信息对齐异质的异常线索，使其能够在统一的训练无关框架内直接融合。基于该校准，检测器结合互补的冻结特征线索，同时应对逻辑异常和结构异常，无需额外训练或部件级标注。

Result: 在MVTec-LOCO上，图像级AUROC在逻辑异常上为89.0，结构异常上为95.9，平均92.5，是所有对比训练无关检测器中的最佳结果。与需要网络训练或部件标注的方法相比仍具竞争力。其结构变体在MVTec-AD上达到99.1的图像AUROC，匹配PatchCore，证明该校准泛化能力超越逻辑异常检测。

Conclusion: 该方法表明通过正常集校准，可以在无需训练和监督的条件下将计数能力引入训练无关的异常检测框架，实现逻辑异常与结构异常的统一处理，且校准机制具有良好的泛化性。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection must handle two distinct defect families: structural anomalies, which manifest as local texture corruptions, and logical anomalies, which violate global rules on object count, composition, or arrangement. Existing detectors typically favor one family at the expense of the other. In particular, training-free methods effectively exploit frozen representations but lack an explicit notion of object count, while methods that reason about counts usually rely on category-specific component modeling. We show that counting ability can be introduced into training-free anomaly detection without additional training or part-level supervision. Our key idea is a normal-set calibration that aligns heterogeneous anomaly cues using statistics from normal images, enabling their direct fusion within a unified training-free framework. Built upon this calibration, our detector combines complementary frozen cues to address both logical and structural anomalies. On MVTec-LOCO, our method achieves image-level AUROCs of 89.0 and 95.9 on logical and structural anomalies, respectively, yielding a 92.5 average---the best among training-free detectors in our comparison. It remains competitive with methods requiring network training or part annotations, while its structural variant matches PatchCore on MVTec-AD (99.1 image-AUROC), suggesting that the proposed calibration generalizes beyond logical anomaly detection.

</details>


### [62] [VoxelFix: Post-Hoc Semantic Correction of Completed 3D Voxel Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05114)
*Sunesh Praveen Raja Sundarasami,Taehyoung Kim,Johannes Scherer,Tomaž Cotič,Sivasubiramaniam Subbiah,Andreas Greiner,Paul Spannaus,Sebastian Houben*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图的模型（方法名被占位符替代），在保持几何和占据状态不变的前提下，直接从已完成的3D语义地图中纠正体素标签，无需依赖原始观测。在OccuFly数据集上，该方法对四种独立训练的2D分割模型生成的完成地图一致地提升了4.23–5.00个百分点的mIoU，并能泛化到训练环境之外的空中场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动构建的语义3D地图中，感知与建图管线中的错误会残留在地图中，降低下游自主任务的可靠性。现有3D语义地图细化方法要么依赖原始观测、要么将占据预测与语义预测耦合、要么在完成后的地图上施加非学习的局部正则化，均存在局限。作者希望探索一种事后语义纠正（post-hoc semantic correction）方案，直接从已完成地图中恢复语义精度。

Method: 提出图模型（\method），根据局部几何结构和相邻体素的语义信息纠正体素标签。训练数据对通过根据上游地图中观察到的类别混淆，对标注的OccuFly地图中连续区域进行污染来构造。模型在由四种独立训练2D分割模型预测生成的已完成OccuFly地图上评估。

Result: \method在各设置下一致地将mIoU提升4.23–5.00个百分点，增益在评估的语义类别中广泛分布，其中对tree、roof和wall的提升尤为显著。在独立重建的分布外（out-of-distribution）空中场景上的结果还表明，学习的纠正可泛化到训练未见过的环境。

Conclusion: 只利用已完成地图中的几何与语义信息，即可进行有效的事后语义纠正来提升语义地图质量，且该方法具有良好的跨场景泛化能力，为不依赖原始观测的3D语义地图细化提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Semantic 3D maps are increasingly constructed automatically for aerial robotics by integrating learned semantic predictions into 3D representations. While this avoids costly manual 3D annotation, errors in the perception and mapping pipeline can persist in the resulting map, reducing its reliability for downstream autonomous tasks. Existing 3D semantic map refinement methods either rely on the original observations, treat occupancy as part of the prediction problem, or apply non-learned local regularization to completed maps. Instead, we study post-hoc semantic correction, asking whether semantic accuracy can be recovered directly from the completed map while keeping its geometry and occupancy fixed. We introduce \method, a graph-based model that corrects voxel labels based on local geometry and neighboring semantic information. To obtain training pairs, we corrupt contiguous regions of annotated OccuFly maps according to class confusions observed in upstream maps. We evaluate \method on completed OccuFly maps generated from predictions of four independently trained 2D segmentation models. \method consistently improves mIoU by 4.23--5.00 percentage points, with gains broadly distributed across the evaluated semantic classes and particularly strong improvements for tree, roof, and wall. Results on an independently reconstructed out-of-distribution aerial scene further suggest that the learned correction can transfer beyond the environments seen during training.

</details>


### [63] [WeAgent-MMGenEdit: A Full-Stack Recipe for Multimodal Agentic Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05171)
*Hui Zhang,Zongkai Liu,Liqiang Niu,Juntao Liu,Han Li,Zhen Cao,Wenchao Chen,Chengduo Zhao,Fandong Meng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了WeAgent-MMGenEdit，一个面向知识密集型图像生成与编辑的全栈式智能体方案，包括多模态运行时（WeAgent-Harness）、可扩展的数据构建管线、双语基准（WeBench-MMGenEdit）及双端后训练方法。该方法通过持久化证据管理、专用验证与集成工具，将检索到的多模态证据组织为密集载体，配合三层次可验证清单的SFT和RL后训练，使30B总参数（3B激活）的策略模型性能接近1T参数智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成与编辑模型在需要外部世界知识的提示下不可靠，原因有两个：一是有界和长尾的参数化知识限制了直接生成或先推理后生成的方法；二是现有智能体式方法虽引入检索工具，但仍受限于验证不充分、策略模型过载以及检索到的文本与视觉证据整合薄弱等问题。

Method: 作者提出了完整的技术栈方案，包含四个核心组件：(1) WeAgent-Harness——多模态运行时，具备持久化证据管理和专门的验证与集成工具，将检索的多模态证据组织为密集载体；(2) 可扩展的数据构建管线，自动合成提示并收集智能体轨迹，共生成23K监督微调轨迹和14.7K带三层次可验证清单的强化学习任务；(3) WeBench-MMGenEdit——涵盖知识密集型图像生成与多图像编辑的双语基准；(4) 基于SFT和RL的双端后训练方法，同时优化智能体策略与图像后端。

Result: WeAgent-MMGenEdit使得一个总参数量30B、激活参数量3B的策略模型在基准上超越同规模策略模型，并接近1T参数智能体的性能水平，验证了检索增强与后训练方法在知识密集型图像生成与编辑任务中的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将检索到的多模态证据以高密度方式组织管理，并配合策略模型与图像后端的两端后训练，能有效解决智能体图像生成与编辑中知识获取、验证和集成三个关键瓶颈，为知识密集型视觉生成任务提供了一条可扩展且数据高效的可行路径。

Abstract: Image generation and editing models have advanced rapidly, yet remain unreliable when prompts require external world knowledge. Bounded and long-tail parametric knowledge prevents direct or reason-then-generate approaches from recovering the required facts and visual appearances. Existing agentic generation and editing methods mitigate this limitation with retrieval tools, yet remain constrained by insufficient visual verification, overloaded policy models, and weak integration of retrieved textual and visual evidence. To address these limitations, we present WeAgent-MMGenEdit, a full-stack recipe including a multimodal harness, a scalable data construction pipeline, a comprehensive benchmark, and post-training methods for the agent policy and image backend. We first introduce WeAgent-Harness, a multimodal runtime with persistent evidence management and dedicated verification and integration tools that organize retrieved multimodal evidence into a dense carrier. Upon this, we develop a scalable pipeline for prompt synthesis and agentic trajectory collection, yielding 23K supervised trajectories and 14.7K RL tasks with three-layer verifiable checklists. We further introduce WeBench-MMGenEdit, a bilingual benchmark covering both knowledge-intensive image generation and multi-image editing. Finally, a two-sided post-training recipe based on SFT and RL improves the agent policy and image backend. Together, WeAgent-MMGenEdit enables a 30B-total/3B-active policy to outperform similarly sized policy models and approach the performance of a 1T-parameter agent.

</details>


### [64] [SMILE: Self-Explainable Multimodal Information Bottleneck for Medical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05174)
*Yuqing Yang,Alexander Schmatz,Zhaozhao Ma,Changkyu Choi,Robert Jenssen,Shujian Yu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于信息瓶颈框架的自解释多模态医学诊断统一学习范式,通过矩阵Renyi α阶熵函数实现可解释性与诊断性能的联合优化,在多模态医学数据集上取得显著性能提升(如iCTCF数据集上绝对精度提升9.1个百分点),并提供模态感知的透明特征相关性解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗AI诊断中可解释性日益关键,但现有多数可解释方法为事后(post-hoc)解释且仅适用于单模态数据,难以满足多模态临床诊断场景的需求。本文旨在解决该问题,提出自解释的(self-explainable)多模态诊断新范式。

Method: 将自解释多模态诊断问题形式化为信息瓶颈(IB)框架,提出统一学习范式,联合优化诊断性能与模态特定可解释性,通过识别各模态中对诊断决策贡献最大的信息元素来实现。为获得可处理且稳定的优化过程,在编码器具有充分表达能力的假设下,采用基于矩阵的Renyi α阶熵函数。

Result: 在覆盖异构模态的代表性医学数据集上进行了大量实验,方法持续取得强诊断性能,其中在iCTCF数据集上实现9.1个百分点的绝对准确率提升。学习得到的解释提供透明且模态感知的特征相关性洞察,改善可解释性与泛化性能。

Conclusion: 本文提出的IB框架下的自解释多模态诊断方法既保证了优异的诊断性能,又提供了模态级透明可解释性,同时增强了模型的泛化能力,为多模态医学诊断中的可解释AI提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Explainability is increasingly seen as a crucial requirement in AI-based medical diagnosis, particularly in safety-critical clinical decision-making. Most existing explainability methods in healthcare operate in a post-hoc manner and are predominantly designed for unimodal data, which limits their applicability in increasingly prevalent multimodal diagnostic settings. This paper addresses the problem of self-explainable multimodal diagnosis by formulating it within the information bottleneck (IB) framework. We propose a unified learning paradigm that jointly optimizes predictive performance and modality-specific explainability by identifying the most informative elements inside each modality that contribute to diagnostic decisions. To enable tractable and stable optimization, we employ a matrix-based Renyi's $α$-order entropy functional under the assumption of sufficiently expressive encoders. Extensive experiments on representative medical datasets spanning heterogeneous modalities demonstrate that the proposed method consistently achieves strong diagnostic performance, including an absolute accuracy improvement of 9.1 percentage points on the iCTCF dataset. Moreover, the learned explanations provide transparent and modality-aware insights into feature relevance, thereby improving both the explainability and generalization.

</details>


### [65] [Conserved Immune Topology Improves Pathology Foundation Model Generalization for Cross-Cancer MSI-H Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.05182)
*Dasari Naga Raju*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Conserved Immune Topology (CIT)，一种轻量级空间表示方法，通过免疫拓扑特征增强病理基础模型嵌入，实现跨癌种MSI-H预测的零样本泛化，在跨中心和跨癌种设置下将TransMIL AUC从0.6627提升至0.7161。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 病理基础模型结合多实例学习在单癌种队列中表现良好，但由于器官特异性的组织学和结构差异，跨癌种泛化问题仍未解决。现有的方法缺乏利用免疫微环境空间组织信息来弥合不同癌种之间差异的机制。

Method: CIT方法包括：1) 使用无监督聚类识别免疫相关切片（tiles）；2) 从冻结的基础模型嵌入和切片坐标中编码三级淋巴结构、瘤周免疫反应、多尺度肿瘤浸润淋巴细胞密度以及免疫-肿瘤混合度等生物学驱动的免疫描述符；3) 无需标注或目标域数据，直接增强基础模型嵌入用于MIL聚合。

Result: 在CPTAC-COAD和TCGA-STAD队列上评估，CIT在零样本跨癌种转移中将TransMIL AUC从0.6627提升至0.7161，绝对增益0.0534（p=0.003），且在所有三种MIL聚合器上均取得一致性改进。

Conclusion: 空间免疫拓扑可能提供器官不变的表示用于MSI-H预测，支持病理基础模型在跨癌种场景下的泛化能力。

Abstract: Pathology foundation models integrated with multiple instance learning achieve competitive accuracy within single-cancer cohorts, yet cross-cancer generalization remains unresolved due to organ-specific histological and architectural differences.
